- Command: `node cwplans/tools/check-factoidal.mjs`
- Method: Re-tests the known @factoidal/core faults and behaviours on tiny in-memory datasets and prints STILL or FIXED for each: BNODE() labels with a colon, serialize() dropping their quads, parse() of toNQuads() dropping lines, SELECT BNODE() values with a _: prefix, blank nodes renamed per query, no default-graph view of named graphs. Also the store points of Factoidal issue 697: one block per (predicate, graph), zone keys sorted by length first, a COUNT over a subquery planning every block (small stores packed in a temporary folder and removed), the unexported README import path, and an IRI with several "#" accepted.
- Rules:
  - Run after every Factoidal upgrade; remove a workaround in web-idioms.mjs or elsewhere only when its line says FIXED.
  - No network; the store checks pack small stores in a temporary folder and remove it; nothing in the repository is written.
- Inputs:
  - **factoidal** (@factoidal/core 0.7.1, Apache-2.0): npm @factoidal/core (local); parse, query (SELECT, CONSTRUCT), serialize, toNQuads on tiny in-memory cases
- Outputs: none (prints only)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: check; after: nothing

#### 76. `probe-imagery-coverage` (tools/probe-imagery-coverage.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/probe-imagery-coverage.mjs [--offline <date>]`
- Method: For each study area box (Canary Wharf, Isle of Dogs south, Limehouse, Rotherhithe / SE16, the zone): OpenAerialMap /meta by box; Panoramax /api/search by box, split in quarters while an answer is full (500); KartaView /2.0/photo at the points of a 200 m grid with a 145 m radius (areas only), halving the radius on a timeout; EA survey catalogue search by polygon. Answers are cut to the fields used. Then the operation lift-imagery-coverage (kgx-ops Flow) turns the answer files into the kgx graph version coverage-imagery and names it in kgx/external-heads.json.
- Rules:
  - Share-alike sources (Panoramax, KartaView, Mapillary) give counts only; their raw answers stay local.
  - Uploader and photographer names are not kept.
  - About one request a second; KartaView at most 150 items a page.
- Inputs:
  - **openaerialmap** (OpenAerialMap catalogue API, CC BY 4.0)
  - **panoramax** (Panoramax federated STAC search, per picture; CC BY-SA 4.0 seen (share-alike: counts only committed))
  - **kartaview** (KartaView photo API v2, CC BY-SA 4.0 (share-alike: counts only committed))
  - **ea-survey-catalogue** (Environment Agency survey download catalogue search, OGL v3.0)
  - **mapillary** (Mapillary Graph API, CC BY-SA 4.0 (share-alike))
- Outputs:
  - [coverage/raw/*/openaerialmap-*.json](coverage/raw/*/openaerialmap-*.json)
  - [coverage/raw/*/ea-survey-*.json](coverage/raw/*/ea-survey-*.json)
  - [coverage/raw/*/mapillary.json](coverage/raw/*/mapillary.json)
  - `data/raw/coverage/<date>/panoramax-*.json, kartaview-*.json (CC BY-SA: not committed)` (local, not committed)
  - **kgx** (the cwplans knowledge graph in danbri/londat kgx/, per graph, as its meta graph states; includes OSM-derived data (ODbL) and data crawled for scoping)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing

#### 77. `contrib-photos` (tools/contrib-photos.mjs)

- Command: `FACADE_PY=<venv python> node cwplans/tools/contrib-photos.mjs <set>`
- Method: Operation rectify-facade-patches: for each facade region in photos.json, facade.py rectifies it (LSD segments, RANSAC vanishing points, homography) and measure.py cuts the measuring box and gives bay and floor periods (autocorrelation) and three colours (k-means in Lab); the patch is written to rect/<id>.jpg. Operation lift-contrib-photos: photos.json (identifications with evidence and confidence, judged facade notes, model heights) and the patch graph give a schema.org Photograph per photo and a node per building. Both graphs are named in kgx/external-heads.json. Operation cut-facade-tiles: the photos.json tiles (a cut of a rectified patch, a mirrored half for a symmetric face, or a vector pattern in metres with judged colours) -> 256 px PNG tiles (and SVG for a pattern) in tiles/ and the graph facade-tiles-<set>.
- Rules:
  - Inputs by SHA-256: the photos, photos.json, facade.py and measure.py.
  - Identifications and facade patterns are judgement, written in photos.json with their evidence; the tool only lifts them.
  - Measured colours are as photographed (shade, backlight).
- Inputs:
  - **contrib-photos** (Photos given to the project by the owner, CC0 1.0)
  - **osm** (OpenStreetMap, ODbL 1.0)
  - [registry/sources/facades/tools/facade.py](registry/sources/facades/tools/facade.py)
  - [registry/sources/facades/tools/measure.py](registry/sources/facades/tools/measure.py)
  - [tools/facade-tile.py](tools/facade-tile.py)
- Outputs:
  - **kgx** (the cwplans knowledge graph in danbri/londat kgx/, per graph, as its meta graph states; includes OSM-derived data (ODbL) and data crawled for scoping)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: nothing

#### 99. `render-structured-data` (tools/render-structured-data.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node cwplans/tools/render-structured-data.mjs [--retry-failed] [--storefinder-only] [--build-only]`
- Method: Renders every web page the registry links to in headless Chromium and extracts from the rendered DOM: every JSON-LD script text as found, microdata (WHATWG algorithm) as JSON, and RDFa 1.1 as N-Triples. The same extractor runs over the server HTML of the same visit with scripts off, to mark blocks that only scripts made. For chain branches with no store page, searches the brand's store finder with the branch postcode and renders the branch page it links to.
- Rules:
  - Skipped: FSA ratings pages, OSM, Wikidata, Wikipedia (CC BY-SA), Companies House, Land Registry, register pages, social networks, the live canarywharf.com (Imperva challenge).
  - robots.txt per RFC 9309 (token glitchcan-cwplans, else *); an unreachable robots.txt means no visit; the failure is classed by its cause (dns, tls, timeout, 5xx).
  - Order: store pages, then pages the plain crawl got no structured data from, then the rest; Internet Archive CWG copies read from cache with scripts off.
  - A cookie banner is clicked only when the page shows no structured data and a known consent button is visible. No logins, no forms other than a store-finder search box with a UK postcode.
  - Store finder: a result link is followed only when its text, URL or card names the branch postcode, a street or mall phrase of the branch, or (for a brand with one branch here) Canary Wharf.
  - Blocks over 200 kB are dropped and counted.
  - User-Agent: a desktop Chrome string plus " glitchcan-cwplans/0.1 (+https://github.com/danbri/londat)"; robots.txt fetched with Node (15 s timeout), else through Chromium.
  - Each page: wait for load and network idle, at most 20 s (CAP_MS), then 1.5 s (LATE_MS) for late JSON-LD.
  - Store finders: one postcode search per brand and postcode; the postcode is the branch's own, else E14 4QT (owner rule 2026-10-03: "we are permitted per industry convention to submit storefinder forms with UK postcodes"). No other forms.
  - Resumable: a URL with a stored result in data/raw/rendered/ is not rendered again unless --retry-failed and its failure was transient.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/branches.json](registry/sources/brands/branches.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json](registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/wikidata-brands.json](registry/sources/brands/wikidata-brands.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/charities.json](registry/sources/registers/charities.json)
  - [registry/sources/web/site-facts.json](registry/sources/web/site-facts.json)
  - [tools/structured-dom.mjs](tools/structured-dom.mjs)
  - `data/raw/crawl/<host>/robots.json and <hash>.html.gz (robots rules and server HTML of the plain crawl, when present)` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/cwg/pages/` (local, not committed)
  - **web-structured-data** (schema.org structured data, none stated (all rights reserved by default); the site owners publish this markup for search engines): occupants[].website, .contact:website, .cwg_website, .store_url (buildings.json); branches[].website, .cwg_website, .store_url; storelocator checked[].store_url; cwg-directory directory[].website; charities records[].website (registered charities); brand sites from Wikidata P856 for store finders; robots.txt once per host (Node fetch, else Chromium), then one headless Chromium page load per URL; robots.txt checked for every main-frame navigation; at most 3 pages at once, one per host, at least 2 s apart; images, media and fonts blocked; store finders: home page, up to three finder pages, one postcode search per brand and postcode, the branch page
- Outputs:
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/index.json](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/index.json)
  - `data/raw/rendered/<host>/<sha1(url)[0:16]>.html.gz (rendered DOM), .json (result), robots.json; data/raw/rendered/_storefinder/` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: `crawl-sites`
- Hand judgement: The skip list and the consent-button list are written in the tool; store-finder searches follow the owner rule of 2026-10-03.

#### 100. `extract-structured-data` (tools/extract-structured-data.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/extract-structured-data.mjs`
- Method: Cleans each JSON-LD block (jsonld-clean.mjs: wrappers, comments, trailing and missing commas, control characters, invalid escapes, concatenated values, HTML entities, remote schema.org contexts inlined as {"@vocab": "https://schema.org/"}, @graph unwrapped, http://schema.org/ normalised; every repair counted by class), converts it with Factoidal jsonldToRdf, converts microdata JSON to RDF (schema.org vocabulary), parses RDFa N-Triples with Factoidal, loads one named graph per page, writes all.nq, then runs SPARQL with Factoidal for types, names, telephones, opening hours and their specifications, addresses, geo and events, and attributes each node to the registry entities that link to the page.
- Rules:
  - Scope branch, confidence high: the node's postcode equals the entity's, or its geo is within 300 m of the building.
  - Scope branch, confidence medium: the node has no address and the page is the branch's own page (a store_url field, a store-finder result, or a URL path that names the place).
  - Scope branch, confidence low: the node's postcode is another E14/E20 postcode (a sibling branch is possible).
  - Scope chain: a chain branch's general page with no address on the node (chain-wide facts, not branch facts). Scope organisation: the same for a non-chain entity's own site.
  - Nodes with an address or position elsewhere are not attributed and are counted.
  - Opening hours to OSM opening_hours: days grouped, 23:59 closes become 24:00, opens = closes = 00:00 is 'off', dated specifications become date-range rules; unparsed text is kept raw with the problem listed.
- Inputs:
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/index.json](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/index.json)
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/branches.json](registry/sources/brands/branches.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json](registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/charities.json](registry/sources/registers/charities.json)
  - [tools/jsonld-clean.mjs](tools/jsonld-clean.mjs)
  - **factoidal** (@factoidal/core 0.7.1, Apache-2.0): npm @factoidal/core 0.7.1 (local); jsonldToRdf, parse (N-Triples, N-Quads), query (SPARQL 1.1 SELECT over named graphs)
- Outputs:
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/all.nq.gz](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/all.nq.gz)
  - [registry/sources/web/structured-facts.json](registry/sources/web/structured-facts.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `render-structured-data`

#### 101. `web-idioms` (tools/web-idioms.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/web-idioms.mjs`
- Method: For each page graph (as a default graph, schema.org namespace variants mapped to https://schema.org/), finds candidate nodes for each idiom by SPARQL, checks the idiom's core ShEx shape and each form shape with Factoidal, runs the SPARQL forms, and rewrites the content idioms to the canonical layer with SPARQL CONSTRUCT.
- Rules:
  - Shapes are open: they recognise an idiom and its forms, they do not reject data.
  - A node counts for an idiom when its core shape holds; forms are counted per node.
  - New nodes in rewrites get IRIs from a SHA-1 of their text, not BNODE() (Factoidal 0.7.1 serialize drops BNODE() nodes).
- Inputs:
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/all.nq.gz](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/all.nq.gz)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/idioms.json](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/idioms.json)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/idioms.shex](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/idioms.shex)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/rewrites](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/rewrites)
  - **factoidal** (@factoidal/core 0.7.1, Apache-2.0): npm @factoidal/core 0.7.1 (local); parse (N-Quads), graphs, query (SPARQL SELECT, ASK, CONSTRUCT), shexValidate, serialize
- Outputs:
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/page-idioms.json](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/page-idioms.json)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/summary.json](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/summary.json)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/canonical.nq.gz](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/canonical.nq.gz)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `extract-structured-data`

#### 102. `web-coref` (tools/web-coref.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/web-coref.mjs`
- Method: Reads name, telephone, sameAs, postcode and coordinates of every organisation and place description; applies the key rules in order (same IRI; same site and name, for places also the postcode; same sameAs URL; same telephone; same postcode and name) and writes each link in the named graph of its rule; groups by union; flags a branch page that links to more than one registry building.
- Rules:
  - Telephone and sameAs join only descriptions of the same kind (organisation with organisation, place with place).
  - Two places with postcodes that disagree are never linked, except by an identical IRI.
  - Empty and bare social-network URLs are not keys.
  - A page's registry keys belong to a description only when it is the one place on that page.
- Inputs:
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/all.nq.gz](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/all.nq.gz)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/idioms.json](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/idioms.json)
  - **factoidal** (@factoidal/core 0.7.1, Apache-2.0): npm @factoidal/core 0.7.1 (local); parse (N-Quads), query (SPARQL SELECT over named graphs), serialize
- Outputs:
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/coref/sameas.nq.gz](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/coref/sameas.nq.gz)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/coref/descriptions.json](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/coref/descriptions.json)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/coref/entities.json](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/coref/entities.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `extract-structured-data`, `web-idioms`

#### 103. `cwg-directory-typed` (tools/cwg-directory-typed.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/cwg-directory-typed.mjs`
- Method: Reads each archived canarywharf.com page's WebPage name, datePublished, dateModified and breadcrumb; joins it to the directory record by URL; gives a schema.org type from the CWG kind and category by a fixed table; adds the registry buildings that link to the page.
- Rules:
  - Types come from the CWG category table, not from the publisher (the pages type only the publisher).
  - first_listed is datePublished and last_edited is dateModified of the archived page.
- Inputs:
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/all.nq.gz](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/all.nq.gz)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json)
  - **factoidal** (@factoidal/core 0.7.1, Apache-2.0): npm @factoidal/core 0.7.1 (local); parse (N-Quads), query (SPARQL SELECT over named graphs), serialize
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory-typed.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory-typed.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory-typed.nq](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory-typed.nq)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `extract-structured-data`

#### 104. `probe-site-search` (tools/probe-site-search.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node cwplans/tools/probe-site-search.mjs [--offline]`
- Method: Reads every SearchAction template in the harvest; per site picks one template with a placeholder; fills it with a nonsense control query, "Canary Wharf" and "E14 5AB"; fetches each result page once; counts only the same-site links (assets left out) and mentions that a place query adds over the control; classes each site (finds a branch page, finds other place content, no place result, results drawn by script, robots.txt disallows, blocked, no page); marks the branch-like hits not yet in the harvest.
- Rules:
  - Politeness: robots.txt per host (cached crawl rules, else fetched), the project User-Agent, one request at a time per host, 1.1 s apart.
  - Only GET URLs that the site publishes as its own SearchAction; no forms, no login.
  - Kept: status, counts, JSON-LD types, result URLs. No page text; the raw result pages stay in data/raw/site-search/ (not committed).
- Inputs:
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/all.nq.gz](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/all.nq.gz)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages)
  - **web-crawl** (web pages linked from the registry: occupant and brand websites, brand store pages, and the Canary Wharf Group directory pages as Internet Archive copies, none stated (all rights reserved by default))
  - **factoidal** (@factoidal/core 0.7.1, Apache-2.0): npm @factoidal/core 0.7.1 (local); parse (N-Quads), query (SPARQL SELECT of SearchAction templates)
- Outputs:
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/search/probe.json](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/search/probe.json)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: `extract-structured-data`
- Hand judgement: The branch-like path pattern and the wall pattern are chosen by hand in the tool; a hit list was checked by hand on 2026-10-06 (about a third are blog or news posts).

#### 105. `cwg-hours` (tools/cwg-hours.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/cwg-hours.mjs`
- Method: Reads the seven-row hours table of each cached CWG directory page (Internet Archive copy saved by fetch-cwg.mjs): 9am, 7.30am, 12noon, Midnight, Closed, Open 24hrs, Coming Soon; groups days with the same hours into OSM opening_hours text.
- Rules:
  - A closing time at or before the opening time runs past midnight.
  - A row that is not a time ("109am", "4m", "12pm - 10") is listed in problems and left out, never guessed.
  - All coming soon: no hours.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json)
  - **cwg-site** (Canary Wharf Group directory pages, none stated (all rights reserved by default))
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-hours.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-hours.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `cwg-directory-typed`

#### 106. `hours-by-place` (tools/hours-by-place.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/hours-by-place.mjs`
- Method: Puts every hours text on a 336-slot half-hour week with docklands/opening-hours.js (the 3D page's reader) and the canonical schema.org hours with their dayOfWeek, opens and closes; groups places by area (CWG mall, street-level estate, Wood Wharf, postcode sector outside the CWG list) and kind (coarse: food and drink, shop, service; fine for CWG from schema.org types); medians and shares per group; stratified permutation tests (5,000, fixed seed) of the mall effect on hours per week and weekday closing time within a fine kind; agreement (share of open slots in common) for the same occupant across CWG, OSM and web; postcode unit to mall from the whole CWG list (one mall in at least 80% of 3 or more entries) to place web branches by their parsed postcode.
- Rules:
  - Dated special hours (validFrom) are not the weekly pattern and are left out.
  - An occupant in the CWG list is counted once, from its CWG page; its OSM and web hours are used only for the agreement check.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-hours.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-hours.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory-typed.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory-typed.json)
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/canonical.nq.gz](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/canonical.nq.gz)
  - [docklands/opening-hours.js](docklands/opening-hours.js)
  - **factoidal** (@factoidal/core 0.7.1, Apache-2.0): npm @factoidal/core 0.7.1 (local); parse (N-Quads), query (SPARQL SELECT of branch, postcode and OpeningHoursSpecification)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/web/hours-by-place.json](registry/sources/web/hours-by-place.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `cwg-hours`, `web-idioms`

#### 107. `mall-plan-evidence` (tools/mall-plan-evidence.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/mall-plan-evidence.mjs`
- Method: For each mall: the anchor is the median position of the registry buildings its CWG entries link to; each CWG entry is matched by normalised name (or brand) to the nearest OSM shop, amenity, office or leisure point within 250 m; the CWG level (signage number) is compared with OSM level:ref, else level; footways with a level and indoor ways within 250 m are kept as polylines in metres from the anchor.
- Rules:
  - Read level:ref before level (fault F48).
  - No unit outlines are made: positions are OSM points.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory-typed.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory-typed.json)
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - **osm** (OpenStreetMap, ODbL 1.0)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/mall-plan-evidence.json](registry/sources/brands/mall-plan-evidence.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `cwg-directory-typed`, `registry-osm`
- Hand judgement: The name normalisation (dropping the mall name and common words) is chosen by hand; a wrong branch of a chain can be matched when two are within 250 m.

#### 108. `join-web-facts` (tools/join-web-facts.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/join-web-facts.mjs`
- Method: Adds the facts read from the entities' own web pages (schema.org opening hours, telephone, events, menu, price range, cuisine) to the building occupants as occupants[].web, with the page, the fetch date, the scope and the confidence of the match.
- Rules:
  - Keys: "cwb-NNNN|name" (occupant name in that building, case and accents folded), "branch:<brand QID>@<element>" (the occupant's OSM element, the building's OSM way, fhrs/<FSA id> or a bare FSA id, cwg/<slug>), "charity:<number>", "cwg:<directory slug>".
  - One record per occupant: branch scope beats organisation beats chain; then high, medium, low confidence.
  - A fact record with no hours, phone, events, menu, price range or cuisine is not joined; records that match no occupant are counted (summary.joins.web_facts).
  - Run after build-registry (which rewrites buildings.json without web facts) and before build-categories and build-atlas.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [registry/sources/web/structured-facts.json](registry/sources/web/structured-facts.json)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `build-registry`, `extract-structured-data`

#### 121. `key-model-buildings` (tools/key-model-buildings.mjs)

- Command: `node --max-old-space-size=6000 cwplans/tools/key-model-buildings.mjs`
- Method: Operation key-model-buildings: every OSM building and building:part outline of the clip is put through the build's own steps (build-docklands.mjs polysOf, toRings with the focus-box tolerance, poly) and its encoded outline compared with the model's; the build's order (outlines without parts, then parts) aligns the model list with the OSM elements, so equal outlines (a multipolygon's outer that is also a building way) get the right element. Tags come from the full extract (osmium getid; the clip keeps no addresses). The graph holds, per OSM element, its OSM page, model indices, parent building of a part, registry link from the atlas, and the OSM name, house name, address, postcode, building type other than yes, levels and Wikidata item. building-keys.json is its projection for the page, with the SHA-256 of area.js and a fingerprint the page checks.
- Rules:
  - Fails when a model building has no OSM outline in the clip (the clip is not the one area.js was built from).
  - Rerun after build-docklands.mjs: the indices are for one area.js.
- Inputs:
  - [docklands/data/area.js](docklands/data/area.js)
  - `data/raw/docklands/osm-clip.json.gz` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf` (local, not committed)
  - [atlas/data/atlas.json](atlas/data/atlas.json)
  - **osm** (OpenStreetMap, ODbL 1.0)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/building-keys.json](docklands/data/building-keys.json)
  - **kgx** (the cwplans knowledge graph in danbri/londat kgx/, per graph, as its meta graph states; includes OSM-derived data (ODbL) and data crawled for scoping)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `build-docklands`, `build-atlas`

#### 122. `compose-facade-atlas` (tools/compose-facade-atlas.mjs)

- Command: `FACADE_PY=<python with Pillow> node cwplans/tools/compose-facade-atlas.mjs`
- Method: Operation compose-facade-atlas: the registry atlas (slots 0 to 15) and every contributed tile in the next slots (32 in all); each contributed tile's buildings are keyed by OSM id with the model indices of this area.js (from model-building-keys, parts included) and a WGS84 point inside the building for a rebuilt model.
- Rules:
  - Fails past 32 slots (the atlas and the shader hold 32).
  - Rerun after build-facade-atlas.py, contrib-photos.mjs or key-model-buildings.mjs.
- Inputs:
  - [docklands/data/tex/facades-registry.jpg](docklands/data/tex/facades-registry.jpg)
  - [docklands/data/tex/facades-registry.json](docklands/data/tex/facades-registry.json)
  - [docklands/data/area.js](docklands/data/area.js)
  - [tools/facade-tile.py](tools/facade-tile.py)
  - **kgx** (the cwplans knowledge graph in danbri/londat kgx/, per graph, as its meta graph states; includes OSM-derived data (ODbL) and data crawled for scoping)
  - **contrib-photos** (Photos given to the project by the owner, CC0 1.0)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/tex/facades.jpg](docklands/data/tex/facades.jpg)
  - [docklands/data/tex/facades.json](docklands/data/tex/facades.json)
  - **kgx** (the cwplans knowledge graph in danbri/londat kgx/, per graph, as its meta graph states; includes OSM-derived data (ODbL) and data crawled for scoping)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `build-facade-atlas`, `contrib-photos`, `key-model-buildings`

#### 123. `build-kgx` (tools/build-kgx.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/build-kgx.mjs [--no-store]`
- Method: Every step is a logged operation (tools/kgx-ops.mjs, skill cwplans-dataflow) over inputs fixed by content hash. Lift operations turn the source files into one graph version each (facts, buildings with WKT outlines, occupants, cwg with daily opening-hours specifications, web, coref per key rule, mallmap units and facilities, storeguide entries, pipeline provenance from pipeline.jsonld with blank nodes skolemized); partition-by-subject-key cuts each version into parts of about 3,000 triples in Shardborough zone-key order; describe-graph-versions makes meta (void:Dataset per version and part, heads); lift-activity-log makes log; pack-shardborough packs the parts, meta and log (ibk5) and activates the generation. Writes graphs/<name>/<hash16>.nq.gz, log/activities.jsonl and versions.jsonl, heads.json, current.nq.gz, manifest.json and shardborough/ into danbri/londat kgx/. An activity already in the log is not run again; a second run gives the same generation.
- Rules:
  - No blank nodes: every node has an ID under https://kgx.foaf.tv/id/ (lowercase alphanumeric local name, tools/kgx-ids.mjs; the pipeline graph is skolemized). Every IRI term goes through mapLegacy(), so names of the old namespaces in inputs (and external heads named before 2026-10-07, through operation map-legacy-ids) become IDs; vocabularies (cwk:, cwp:) unchanged.
  - serialize() output is checked to have one line per distinct quad; lines read back must hash to their version.
  - Graph versions are immutable and named by their RDFC-1.0 hash; an activity is named by (operation, operation version, input IRIs, parameters) and is not run again.
  - Bump an operation's version when its code changes.
  - The log graph leaves out log lifts and store packs, so an unchanged pipeline gives the same store generation.
  - Only the current Shardborough generation is kept in the folder; git keeps history.
- Inputs:
  - [docklands/facts.json](docklands/facts.json)
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [atlas/data/atlas.json](atlas/data/atlas.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory-typed.nq](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory-typed.nq)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory-typed.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory-typed.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-hours.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-hours.json)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/canonical.nq.gz](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/idioms/canonical.nq.gz)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/coref/sameas.nq.gz](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/coref/sameas.nq.gz)
  - [../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/coref/descriptions.json](../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/coref/descriptions.json)
  - [pipeline.jsonld](pipeline.jsonld)
  - **kgx** (the cwplans knowledge graph in danbri/londat kgx/, per graph, as its meta graph states; includes OSM-derived data (ODbL) and data crawled for scoping)
  - **livingmap-cwg** (Living Map service behind https://map.canarywharf.com/, Canary Wharf Group / Living Map; no published licence or terms found on 2026-10-06; basemap tiles © OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL)
  - **cwg-maps** (Canary Wharf Group printed maps and guides from https://canarywharf.com/maps/, Canary Wharf Group; store guide design © Ravenshaw Studios Limited and Paul & Linda Anthony 2026; not an open licence)
  - **factoidal** (@factoidal/core 0.7.1, Apache-2.0): npm @factoidal/core 0.7.1 (local); parse, serialize (N-Quads), jsonldToRdf, fn.hash (RDFC-1.0, for graphs with blank nodes), factoidal pack --layout ibk5 and activate (Shardborough)
- Outputs: none (prints only)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: derive; after: `cwg-hours`, `web-idioms`, `web-coref`, `cwg-maps-tmi`, `cwg-mallmap-tmi`, `probe-imagery-coverage`, `contrib-photos`, `key-model-buildings`, `compose-facade-atlas`

#### 124. `kgx-query` (tools/kgx-query.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/kgx-query.mjs <file.rq | SPARQL> [store dir]`
- Method: Opens a store handle on the kgx Shardborough store with the artifacts the query's plan names (no 64-block cap) and prints the answer as tab-separated rows.
- Rules:
  - Opens only the artifacts the query's plan names.
  - Read-only: writes nothing.
- Inputs:
  - **kgx** (the cwplans knowledge graph in danbri/londat kgx/, per graph, as its meta graph states; includes OSM-derived data (ODbL) and data crawled for scoping)
  - **factoidal** (@factoidal/core 0.7.1, Apache-2.0): npm @factoidal/core 0.7.1 (local); openStore, openStoreHandle ({ sparql }), handle query
- Outputs: none (prints only)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: check; after: `build-kgx`

#### 125. `check-kgx-store` (tools/check-kgx-store.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/check-kgx-store.mjs [--write]`
- Method: Rebuilds the store's input from the part versions, meta and log (its SHA-256 prefix must equal the generation name), runs every kgx/queries/*.rq and cross-subject joins through a store handle and through the in-memory engine, and compares the solution multisets; the joins also on the data with one graph per source.
- Rules:
  - Exit 1 on any difference between the store and the in-memory engine.
  - Refuses a store that was not packed from the current versions.
- Inputs:
  - **kgx** (the cwplans knowledge graph in danbri/londat kgx/, per graph, as its meta graph states; includes OSM-derived data (ODbL) and data crawled for scoping)
  - **factoidal** (@factoidal/core 0.7.1, Apache-2.0): npm @factoidal/core (local); parse, query (in memory); openStore, openStoreHandle
- Outputs:
  - **kgx** (the cwplans knowledge graph in danbri/londat kgx/, per graph, as its meta graph states; includes OSM-derived data (ODbL) and data crawled for scoping)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: check; after: `build-kgx`

### 14. Atlas index

#### 111. `build-atlas` (tools/build-atlas.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/build-atlas.mjs`
- Method: Builds the compact index the atlas page loads first: buildings with outlines and model heights, postcodes with company and home totals, heritage groups, riverbed soundings, wrecks, flood defences, Wikidata items, published levels and overview counts.
- Rules:
  - Model buildings (area.js) belong to a registry building when their vertex mean lies inside its outline; the tallest gives the model height and source.
  - Heritage records in the Docklands box are grouped by position rounded to 5 decimals.
  - Soundings: z_cd minus the chart datum offset interpolated by longitude between 3.20 m and 3.35 m (same rule as build-docklands).
  - Defence crest: actual_ucl, else actual_dcl, else design_ucl; a line is kept when one of its points is in the box.
  - Wikidata items without an English label are left out.
  - Levels: the sourced levels used by the 3D build, CWG structures (slabs, water, dock bed), the deepest and median sounding off the Isle of Dogs, and the 8 tallest named buildings (Wikidata, else OSM, else LiDAR height on the LiDAR ground, or 5 m OD when unknown, marked approx).
  - Rounding: outlines as microdegree deltas (1e-6 degree, about 0.1 m); positions to 6 decimals; heights and levels to 0.01 m; heritage groups by position rounded to 5 decimals.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [registry/categories.json](registry/categories.json)
  - [data/raw/registry/osm-cw.json.gz](data/raw/registry/osm-cw.json.gz)
  - [docklands/data/area.js](docklands/data/area.js)
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
  - [registry/companies-by-postcode.json](registry/companies-by-postcode.json)
  - [registry/homes-by-postcode.json](registry/homes-by-postcode.json)
  - [registry/sources/museums/records-open.json](registry/sources/museums/records-open.json)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_teddington_greenwich_25m.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_teddington_greenwich_25m.geojson)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_greenwich_coalhouse_50m.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_greenwich_coalhouse_50m.geojson)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_hms_belfast_berth.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_hms_belfast_berth.geojson)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_wrecks_obstructions_points.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_wrecks_obstructions_points.geojson)
  - [data/raw/docklands/ea-defences.json.gz](data/raw/docklands/ea-defences.json.gz)
  - [data/raw/docklands/wikidata-items.json.gz](data/raw/docklands/wikidata-items.json.gz)
  - [data/sourced-levels.json](data/sourced-levels.json)
  - [docklands/facts.json](docklands/facts.json)
  - [feeds/feeds.json](feeds/feeds.json)
  - [feeds/events.json](feeds/events.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/branches.json](registry/sources/brands/branches.json)
  - [data-register.json](data-register.json)
- Outputs:
  - [atlas/data/atlas.json](atlas/data/atlas.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: join; after: `build-registry`, `build-categories`, `build-docklands`, `postcodes-build`, `fetch_records`, `fetch_wet-soundings`, `fetch-docklands-defences`, `fetch-docklands-wikidata`, `build-branches`, `registry-osm`, `fetch-raw-grid`
- Faults: [F5](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

### 15. Trees and greens

#### 62. `fetch-trees` (tools/build-trees.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node cwplans/tools/build-trees.mjs fetch [gla|osgs|tpo|tow ...]`
- Method: Downloads the open tree inventories and green-space layers for the Docklands box into data/raw/trees/ (gitignored).
- Rules:
  - One request at a time, at least 2 s apart; retried with a doubling pause on 429 or 5xx.
  - The GLA CSV and the OS ZIP are written as fetched; the planning.data and TOW responses are concatenated pages (features with their properties and geometry).
- Inputs:
  - **gla-public-realm-trees** (GLA London Public Realm Trees, November 2025 release, OGL v3.0): https://data.london.gov.uk/download/2r45m/e62a6a1f-390d-4193-ae32-3aabd9846f36/Borough_tree_list_2025Nov.csv; the whole London Public Realm Trees CSV, November 2025 release (208 MB)
  - **os-open-greenspace** (OS Open Greenspace, version 2026-04, OGL v3.0 (OS OpenData licence: http://os.uk/opendata/licence redirects to OGL v3)): https://api.os.uk/downloads/v1/products/OpenGreenspace/downloads?area=TQ&format=ESRI%C2%AE+Shapefile&redirect; the TQ tile of OS Open Greenspace as ESRI Shapefile (5 MB ZIP)
  - **planning-data-tree** (planning.data.gov.uk dataset 'tree', OGL v3.0): https://www.planning.data.gov.uk/entity.json; dataset=tree, geometry = the model box (WGS84 polygon), geometry_relation=intersects, 500 a page until a short page
  - **fr-tow** (Forest Research National Trees Outside Woodland map V1, London, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/spatialdata/national-trees-outside-woodland-map/ogc/features/v1/collections/FR_TOW_V1_London/items; f=json, bbox = the model box in WGS84, limit=10000, startIndex 0, 10000, 20000
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/trees/gla-public-realm-trees-2025Nov.csv` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/trees/opgrsp_essh_tq.zip` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/trees/planning-data-tree.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/trees/tow-london-box.json.gz` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing

#### 63. `build-trees` (tools/build-trees.mjs)

- Command: `node --max-old-space-size=6000 cwplans/tools/build-trees.mjs`
- Method: Joins the tree inventories, OSM trees and the Trees Outside Woodland lone-tree crowns into one list of trees in model metres, with OS Open Greenspace sites, OSM green areas and TOW canopy groups as green rings.
- Rules:
  - Box: BNG E 532400-539900, N 176700-182300 (fetch-docklands.mjs BOX_BNG); WGS84 to BNG through OSTN15; x = E - 537550, z = -(N - 180300); every value rounded to 0.5 m.
  - Ethics (CLAUDE.md, Data ethics): a tree keeps only position, taxon (scientific and common name), height, crown spread, source and record id. Notes, sponsorship, memorial, dedication, inscription, address, owner and maintainer fields are never read into the payload; each source lists its dropped fields in sources[].dropped.
  - Tree precedence: GLA public realm inventory first; then TPO points (planning.data), OSM natural=tree nodes and TOW Lone Tree crowns, each added only if no tree already kept lies within 3 m (TOW: within the crown radius, at least 3 m).
  - GLA class values ("05 to 10 m") become the middle of the class; "Not recorded" is null. Species "Unknown Tree Species" and common name "Plant" give no species.
  - TPO: records with an end date are left out; the taxon is read only from Tower Hamlets names of the form "Common (Scientific)"; Southwark names (site descriptions, URLs) are dropped.
  - OSM: species, else taxon, else genus (with the :en names); height under 60 m and diameter_crown under 40 m kept.
  - TOW Lone Tree: centroid of the polygon, height = maxht, crown = diameter of a circle with the polygon area (tow_area_m). Group of Trees, Small Woodland and NFI OHC polygons become greens with meanht.
  - Greens are clipped to the box (Sutherland-Hodgman) and simplified (Douglas-Peucker 0.5 m; TOW 1.5 m, its outlines follow the 1 m LiDAR grid); OSM multipolygon inner rings and shapefile holes are kept as holes.
  - Network for the source files: see fetch-trees.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/trees/gla-public-realm-trees-2025Nov.csv` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/trees/opgrsp_essh_tq.zip` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/trees/planning-data-tree.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/trees/tow-london-box.json.gz` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/trees.json](docklands/data/trees.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `fetch-trees`, `fetch-docklands-osm`, `fetch-raw-grid`
- Hand judgement: The 35 m cut-off for "lone trees" that are cranes or structures is applied on the page, not in the file; the source precedence order is a judgement.

#### 65. `fetch-tree-leaf-tags` (tools/build-tree-species.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node cwplans/tools/build-tree-species.mjs fetch [--from saved-overpass-answer.json]`
- Method: Reads the leaf_type and leaf_cycle tags of the OSM natural=tree nodes in the model box (the Greater London PBF extract of build-trees is not committed and build-trees drops these tags). Keeps node id, leaf_type and leaf_cycle only.
- Rules:
  - Only nodes with leaf_type or leaf_cycle are kept: 11,993 of 34,445 (OSM data of 2026-10-09T02:53:01Z).
  - 2026-10-09: overpass-api.de reset the connection and the mail.ru mirror answered 504 to the tool; the file was written with --from from an answer to the same query fetched from the mail.ru mirror an hour before.
  - User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1 (https://github.com/danbri/londat).
- Inputs:
  - **osm** (OpenStreetMap, ODbL 1.0): https://overpass-api.de/api/interpreter, then https://maps.mail.ru/osm/tools/overpass/api/interpreter; [out:json];node["natural"="tree"](model box, WGS84);out tags; (one request; up to 3 rounds over the two servers, 2 s apart)
- Outputs:
  - [data/raw/trees/osm-tree-leaf.json.gz](data/raw/trees/osm-tree-leaf.json.gz)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing

#### 66. `build-tree-species` (tools/build-tree-species.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/build-tree-species.mjs`
- Method: Gives each tree of trees.json a species profile of the 3D page (docklands/tree-species.js in danbri/londat: crown shape, leaf and autumn colours, bark, leaf-out, autumn and leaf-fall days, blossom) and the basis of that profile. A pure function of the three inputs (their SHA-256 are written into the output) and the parameters (waterM 15, waterCell 5).
- Rules:
  - Taxon first: the scientific and common name of the tree (trees.json species) through the ordered rules of tree-species.js profileOf(); basis S for a species or cultivar, G for a genus, family or vague name.
  - Names that say nothing about the tree (New tree pit, Misc group, Mixed species, Unplantable, Mistaken, Vacant, unknown, ?) count as no taxon.
  - No taxon: the setting is waterside if a 5 m cell inside an area.js water polygon lies within 15 m; else wood if inside a trees.json green of kind natural=wood, scrub, landuse=forest or a TOW group or woodland; else park if inside any other green, or if the tree is a TOW lone-tree crown (gardens and courtyards); else street. The profile is drawn from the setting mix (tree-species.js MIXES) with a hash of the record id (stable). Basis R, W, P, T: INFERRED.
  - OSM leaf tags on a tree with no taxon: needleleaved gives conifer (deciduous: conifer-deciduous), palm gives palm, evergreen or semi_evergreen gives evergreen broadleaf, else the setting mix; basis L.
- Inputs:
  - [docklands/data/trees.json](docklands/data/trees.json)
  - [docklands/data/area.js](docklands/data/area.js)
  - [data/raw/trees/osm-tree-leaf.json.gz](data/raw/trees/osm-tree-leaf.json.gz)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/tree-species.json](docklands/data/tree-species.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `build-trees`, `fetch-tree-leaf-tags`

### 16. Imagery and page assets

#### 39. `build-imagery` (tools/build-imagery.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node cwplans/tools/build-imagery.mjs [from-date]`
- Method: Colours each terrain vertex of the Docklands model from the least cloudy recent Sentinel-2 true-colour image.
- Rules:
  - Model metres to BNG to UTM with a Helmert transform (about 2 m, inside a 10 m pixel).
  - Each vertex takes the mean of the pixels within half a terrain cell of it.
  - Network: one STAC search, then HTTP range requests for the pixel window only; User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; no retry; no cache. The scene id and date are written into imagery.js.
  - Colours are 8-bit means, rounded to whole values.
- Inputs:
  - [docklands/data/area.js](docklands/data/area.js)
  - **copernicus-sentinel** (Copernicus Sentinel-2 L2A, Copernicus Sentinel data: free, full and open (attribution)): https://earth-search.aws.element84.com/v1/search; STAC search: collection sentinel-2-l2a intersecting POINT(-0.0195 51.5049) from the from-date (default 120 days before the run) to now, eo:cloud_cover < 10, sorted by cloud cover, limit 1
  - **copernicus-sentinel** (Copernicus Sentinel-2 L2A, Copernicus Sentinel data: free, full and open (attribution)): the scene's 'visual' (TCI) Cloud-Optimised GeoTIFF on S3; HTTP range requests for the pixel window over the model box only
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/imagery.js](docklands/data/imagery.js)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: derive; after: `build-docklands`

#### 40. `build-aerial-rgb2008` (tools/build-aerial.py)

- Command: `python3 cwplans/tools/build-aerial.py rgb2008`
- Method: Decodes the 2008 colour aerial photographs, pastes them onto one canvas over the model box at 3 m a pixel and saves a JPEG.
- Rules:
  - Model box x -5150 to 2350, z -2000 to 3600 (local metres), 3 m a pixel.
  - ECW decoded at every 5th pixel; black (0,0,0) outside the flown area is not pasted; one pixel of overlap between tiles.
- Inputs:
  - **ea-dsm-history** (Environment Agency LiDAR DSM survey tiles 1999 to 2022, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/tiles/collections/survey/vertical_aerial_photography_tiles_rgb/2008/0.4/<tile>; tiles TQ3075, TQ3080, TQ3575, TQ3580 (ECW in ZIP) [manual download]
  - `data/raw/imagery/vertical_aerial_photography_tiles_rgb-2008-0.4-*.zip` (local, not committed)
  - `tools/native/ecw2ppm` (local, not committed)
  - [docklands/data/tex/textures.json](docklands/data/tex/textures.json)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/tex/rgb2008.jpg](docklands/data/tex/rgb2008.jpg)
  - [docklands/data/tex/textures.json](docklands/data/tex/textures.json)
  - `data/raw/imagery/rgb2008.ppm` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: nothing

#### 41. `build-aerial-night2012` (tools/build-aerial.py)

- Command: `python3 cwplans/tools/build-aerial.py night2012`
- Method: As build-aerial-rgb2008 for the 2012 night-time aerial photographs.
- Rules:
  - ECW decoded at every 10th pixel; black outside the flown area is not pasted.
- Inputs:
  - **ea-dsm-history** (Environment Agency LiDAR DSM survey tiles 1999 to 2022, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/tiles/collections/survey/vertical_aerial_photography_tiles_night_time/2012/0.2/<tile>; tiles TQ3075, TQ3080, TQ3575, TQ3580 (ECW in ZIP) [manual download]
  - `data/raw/imagery/vertical_aerial_photography_tiles_night_time-2012-0.2-*.zip` (local, not committed)
  - `tools/native/ecw2ppm` (local, not committed)
  - [docklands/data/tex/textures.json](docklands/data/tex/textures.json)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/tex/night2012.jpg](docklands/data/tex/night2012.jpg)
