  - `data/raw/london-datastore/` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Hand judgement: The harvest shortlist (the HARVEST table in the tool) was chosen by hand from the ranked triage: open, relevant, new, machine-readable, not geometry-only where an alternative exists; the choices and the ranked backlog with reasons are in feeds/london-datastore/README.md. The probe rules (caps, thresholds of D2 and D4, the place list) were set by reading the first full run: one cell naming Deptford or one publisher postcode (City Hall) made a zone dataset of a payments list, so D2 needs 3 distinct zone postcodes and D4 2 names in 3 cells; census table ids (QS101EW) read as postcodes until the UK postcode area list was added. Fatal fires (24yo6, incident postcodes) was added to the sensitive titles by hand.
- Faults: [F22](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F23](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F27](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F28](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F29](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F30](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F31](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 57. `lds-harvest-auto` (tools/lds-harvest-auto.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/tools/lds-harvest-auto.mjs plan [id ...]; NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node cwplans/tools/lds-harvest-auto.mjs run [id ...] [--rank a-b] [--again]; node cwplans/tools/lds-harvest-auto.mjs register`
- Method: Harvests the London Datastore datasets that triage.json lists for harvest, by written rules instead of a hand table: picks the readable resources (one per file stem, best format, size caps, hand rules where the general rules would take too much or the wrong file), reads every row and feature (CSV streamed, large xlsx streamed with ExcelJS, xls/ods with SheetJS, GeoPackage, GeoJSON, shapefiles, zips entry by entry), keeps the zone rows (zone codes, postcodes and sectors in profiled columns, UPRNs, TOIDs, coordinates) and the features that meet the 3D model box, and records the outcome of every dataset in harvest-log.json; triage then states no-zone-rows datasets not relevant (F8b) and unreadable ones deferred (F10b).
- Rules:
  - formats: readable resource formats: gpkg, geojson, zip, csv, tsv, xlsx, xlsm, xls, ods; json and txt only when nothing else is readable. Documents, images, kml/kmz, rds and other binary formats are not read.
  - dedupe: one resource per file stem (file name lower-cased, separators to "-", without the extension and without trailing format words shp, gpkg, geojson, csv, xlsx, xls, gis, data, and the layout words long, wide; a wide file is taken before its long twin): gpkg > geojson > zip > csv > tsv > xlsx > xlsm > xls > ods > json > txt, then the newest; identical md5 = one resource.
  - caps: a resource over 1,200 MB is not read (disk); a dataset with more than 40 stems or 2,500 MB in total takes the newest 12 stems unless a hand rule (HAND) says otherwise. Raw files over 20 MB are deleted after reading.
  - names: datasets about town centres or stations (metadata geo Town Centres or Train Stations, or the title) also match names: a cell equal, after normalising (case, &, punctuation, words such as station, DLR, town centre), to the name of a zone town centre (town-centres harvest, polygon meets the box) or a zone station (TfL StopPoint, NaPTAN metro and rail stations in the box); keys town-centre:<name>, station:<name>; weaker than a code.
  - zone_row: a table row is a zone row when a cell is a zone OA/LSOA/MSOA/ward code (zone-codes.json, any vintage), a zone postcode (ONSPD grid reference in the 3D model box), a zone postcode sector (the sector of a zone postcode, in a sector column) , a zone UPRN in a UPRN column (OS Open UPRN in the box), a zone TOID in a TOID column, or its own coordinates lie in the box (lat/lon or easting/northing columns, judged by value). Ward tables: the City of London row (E09000001) is a zone row (the City is one ward-level unit there). The first matching key is recorded per row (zone_keys).
  - transposed: a sheet whose area codes run across a row (3 or more codes in one of the first 15 rows, and no code in a column to the left below it) is read column-wise: the label columns and the columns of zone codes are kept, every row; zone_keys then name the columns ("column ward:E05009323").
  - header: sheets: the up to 4 rows above the first data row (a row with an area code, a postcode or a coordinate) are header_rows; CSV: the first line.
  - geometry: features (GeoPackage, GeoJSON, shapefile) are kept when they meet the box (walk-london-datastore.mjs harvestGeo); a table whose single header row names coordinate columns becomes points in the .geojson (the row cells are the properties).
  - numbers: numeric cells are kept as numbers; non-integers rounded to 6 significant digits (file size); identifiers (leading zero, E0.. codes, osgb TOIDs) stay text.
  - personal: columns whose name matches e-?mail|phone|tel(ephone)?$|^tel|contact|^fax|mobile|website_contact|^owner_?name|person are dropped (contact details), also under the cwplans exception.
  - outcome: harvested: at least one zone row or feature; no-zone-rows: every row or feature read, none in the zone (then triage states the dataset not-relevant, rule F8b, with the counts as evidence); not-readable: no resource could be read (deferred, F10b); documents-only: the zips hold documents only (deferred, F7); deferred-by-hand: a HAND rule with the reason (deferred, F3c); held-for-owner: not run (owner decision pending; the state is left as it was).
  - clip: a polygon of over 1,000 vertices that reaches beyond the zone (a London-wide boundary) is cut to the 3D model box plus 500 m (Sutherland-Hodgman, ring by ring) and marked clipped_to_zone; smaller geometries are kept whole.
  - as_table: a hand rule may keep rows with coordinates as a table (the LAEI grid summaries are one row per cell, source and year: 33,540 points made 15 MB of GeoJSON for LAEI 2010).
  - thin: a hand rule may thin a regular 20 m grid (LAEI concentrations) to 1 cell in 5 each way (a 100 m grid), by the BNG cell index of the point or of the cell centre; the meta says so.
  - hand rule 2o8ng: the 2019-based tools; the 2016-based xls creators (54-57 MB) are superseded
  - hand rule 2r48w: LAEI 2016: grid emission summaries, the road traffic links (GIS), the 20 m concentration grid thinned to 100 m, population and schools exceeding; the per-link emission workbooks (217-447 MB) not read
  - hand rule e550x: one zip per borough; Newham is the only zone borough in the dataset
  - hand rule 2wwq4: the persons and components-of-change sheets of the three variants; the male and female sheets split the persons rows (13.8 MB with them, single year of age x ward x sex)
  - hand rule 2zp76: 2024-based central projection at ward and MSOA (the low and high variants tripled the output to 10 MB; they stay at the source); the variant zips (0.7-1.5 GB), the borough-level files and the superseded 2022-based workbooks not read
  - hand rule v8o11: the LSOA table; the GIS zip (214 MB) repeats it with geometry
  - hand rule 2964y: the summary workbooks (the RM long tables, 18-70 MB each, cross-tabulate the same counts)
  - hand rule 2r7om: the summary workbooks (the RM long tables, 16-106 MB each, cross-tabulate the same counts)
  - hand rule em95y: LAEI 2006 modelled 20 m grid points thinned to 100 m (105,656 zone points at 20 m: 21 MB)
  - hand rule 298jq: LAEI 2006 modelled values per 20 m grid point: the point read from GRID_ID (gla + easting + northing), thinned to 100 m as em95y
  - hand rule 2jrkd: LAEI 2006 modelled values per 20 m grid point: the point read from GRID_ID (gla + easting + northing), thinned to 100 m as em95y
  - hand rule 2np13: LAEI 2006 modelled values per 20 m grid point: the point read from GRID_ID (gla + easting + northing), thinned to 100 m as em95y
  - hand rule 2ry8d: LAEI 2006 modelled values per 20 m grid point: the point read from GRID_ID (gla + easting + northing), thinned to 100 m as em95y
  - hand rule 2yrpy: LAEI 2006 modelled values per 20 m grid point: the point read from GRID_ID (gla + easting + northing), thinned to 100 m as em95y
  - hand rule e188j: LAEI 2006 modelled values per 20 m grid point: the point read from GRID_ID (gla + easting + northing), thinned to 100 m as em95y
  - hand rule 2ry01: LAEI 2008 concentration maps: five 20 m grid shapefiles of about 150 MB each (millions of cells); reading them whole exhausted the 12 GB heap here (2026-10-04); the newer LAEI 2016 20 m grid is harvested thinned to 100 m, and the 2013 and 2019 releases cover the same air
  - hand rule e6410: LAEI 2010 summary emissions (1 km grid); the 20 m concentrations (555 MB) are not read whole here (the LAEI 2008 grids exhausted the heap) and 2-Emissions (1.7 GB, per source) not read; LAEI 2016 holds a newer 20 m grid
  - hand rule em9mg: LAEI 2008: the folders zip; the two database zips hold the same tables as Access databases
  - hand rule exy6d: LAEI 2013 focus areas: GIS and supporting zips; the images zip (199 MB) not read
  - hand rule exyop: LAEI 2016 focus areas: GIS and supporting zips; the images zip (520 MB) not read
  - hand rule exywx: LAEI 2013: grid emission summaries and minor roads by grid (1 km); the 20 m concentration zips (786 MB and 1.5 GB) are not read whole here (the LAEI 2008 grids exhausted the heap; LAEI 2016 holds a newer 20 m grid); the per-link workbooks (350-390 MB each) and older concentration releases not read
  - hand rule 2g1zq: 2011-geography mid-year estimates; the 2001-geography files and the custom age tools repeat them
  - hand rule ex9jd: the London ward series and the MSOA 2010-2011 file; LAD-level files have no zone rows; the LSOA 2010-2011 csv (746 MB) not read; rds not readable
  - hand rule 2jxq0: kept as tables (5,492 zone permissions: 11.7 MB as GeoJSON points); the permission-level extracts (permissions, non-residential floorspace and bedrooms); the unit-level approvals and completions (3 files, 22,417 zone rows, 40 MB of output) repeat the same permissions unit by unit
  - hand rule exynl: AMR 14 (2018): the chapter tables (town centres, Opportunity Areas); its LDD extracts (approvals, starts, completions, pipeline) are earlier snapshots of the London Development Database, superseded by the 2020 extract (2jxq0) and the Planning London Datahub (tools/build-construction-index.mjs)
  - hand rule 204q6: AMR 15 (2019): the chapter tables; its LDD extracts are earlier snapshots of the London Development Database, superseded by the 2020 extract (2jxq0) and the Planning London Datahub
  - hand rule v8o0m: AMR 13 (2017): LDD pipeline and completions extracts, superseded by the 2020 extract (2jxq0, harvested) and the Planning London Datahub
  - hand rule 2zwnk: Lden (day-evening-night) road and rail noise bands only, coordinates to 5 decimals (about 1 m), vertices closer than 2 m dropped; the LAeq16h and Lnight layers (20,000 more zone polygons, 15 MB in all) repeat the same contours for other periods; the 2018 maps (2017 data) are older than Defra round 4 (2022)
  - hand rule 2zjmn: Local Plan layers that only the local plans hold (tall building locations, SINCs, green grid, open space audit, water space, air quality areas, shopping frontages, employment and office locations, Thames Policy Area, Tideway safeguarding, cycle routes...); left out: EA flood and surface water layers (5.4 MB for one polygon; the EA and planning.data.gov.uk flood zones are held), listed buildings, conservation areas, archaeological priority areas, roads, town centres, CAZ and site allocations (held from their own sources); 5 decimals, vertices closer than 1 m dropped
  - hand rule 29jwj: the 2011 output area outlines only, generalised and clipped to the coastline (BGC; the full-resolution BFC and extent BFE copies repeat them) (the LSOA, MSOA and ward layers of 2011 repeat statistical-boundaries and 20od9); 5 decimals
  - hand rule 2r401: Breathe London AQMesh pods: a time series of sensor readings with the pod position on every row (65 MB CSV; the zone rows came to 42 MB as points); the readings belong to a time-series store, not the repository; the pod positions alone are in the Breathe London network listing
  - hand rule e64on: the London-wide postcode files (2010 and later) and the LSOA summaries, kept as tables; the tab-delimited twins and the 33 per-borough files repeat them (13 MB with the duplicates as points)
  - hand rule 2zj4k: Royal Docks Grant Data: the whole dataset is about the Royal Docks (title and publisher, the Royal Docks Enterprise Zone team); its rows name projects, not areas: every row kept (key "whole dataset")
  - hand rule epr1g: OS Code-Point Open (2014 copy on the Datastore): the zone postcodes with their positions came to 25 MB; the project holds the newer ONSPD August 2026 positions of the same postcodes (postcodes/, zone-codes.json)
  - hand rule e55gn: postcode rows with coordinates kept as a table (8,217 zone postcodes: 1.5 MB as rows, 11.8 MB as GeoJSON points)
  - hand rule 2jkxd: the workbooks (one row per area, years across); the CSVs (89 MB for LSOA) hold the same figures one row per area and year
  - hand rule ep8xy: the time series workbook; the five yearly model workbooks (2011-2015, 8.6 MB each) repeat it month by month
  - hand rule emxjy: 2001 Census commissioned tables at output area: 67 zips, 1.8 GB, hundreds of tables; superseded for current context by the 2011 and 2021 Census (harvested by ward and LSOA); not harvested by hand (size against value)
  - held for the owner 2g980: Shut in lift releases (lift entrapments) attended by LFB: incident records at addresses; the owner decides first
  - held for the owner e68wz: Assembly Member gifts register: named people; the owner decides first
  - held for the owner em8xy: London Fire Brigade Incident Records: incident records at addresses (UPRN, coordinates), special service types include suicide/attempts and medical incidents; same class as 2g980, held for the owner
  - held for the owner 24r65: London Fire Brigade Mobilisation Records: the mobilisations of the incident records em8xy (joined by incident number); held with em8xy for the owner
  - held for the owner 2ogkn: Animal rescue incidents attended by LFB: incident records at addresses (UPRN, coordinates, also at homes); same class as 2g980, held for the owner

Datasets of incident records at addresses (LFB incidents, mobilisations, animal rescues) are held with the lift entrapments for the owner. Outputs were read by hand after each batch (counts, zone keys, sizes).
- Faults: [F22](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F27](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F36](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F37](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F38](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F39](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F40](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F41](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 58. `amend-uprns` (tools/amend-uprns.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node cwplans/tools/amend-uprns.mjs cultural-infrastructure [--refresh]; generic: node cwplans/tools/amend-uprns.mjs --in <file> --uprn-field <f> --id-field <f[,f]> [--ref a:<file>]... [--ref c:<file>]... [--open-uprn <zip|csv>] [--footprints <osm-clip.json.gz>] --out <copy> --amendments <json>`
- Method: Copies a spreadsheet-sourced file and amends only the UPRN cells that a spreadsheet rounded (F22). Every cell is classified (rounded: scientific notation, or 9+ digits ending in 5+ zeros; catalogued only: empty, text, decimal, out of range, padded, duplicate across venues, not in OS Open UPRN, over 150 m from its OS Open UPRN point, and per-layer constant position offsets, F25). Each rounded venue is recovered by the first route that gives exactly one candidate inside the range the rounded value allows: (a) the same dataset in other formats, layers and releases, (b) OS Open UPRN points in the venue's OSM building outline, (c) registers with UPRNs; else (d) unknown with the reason. Writes the amended copy beside the source and an amendments file with one entry per changed cell, every unknown, the catalogue, the rules and the measured precision of routes a and b (each valid 12-digit UPRN rounded to 3 and 6 digits and recovered blind).
- Rules:
  - The source file is read, never written; a value that is not in a rounded class is never changed.
  - Range: Excel rounds to k significant digits; the true value lies within half a unit of the last kept digit (1.00023E+11 -> [100022500000, 100023500000); plain 200000000000 -> k = non-zero prefix -> [150000000000, 250000000000)); rounded forms of the same venue in other releases narrow it by intersection.
  - Venue: rows with the same normalised name within 50 m are one venue; candidates are pooled and every row gets the same answer.
  - Route a: same normalised name (or one contains the other, 8+ characters) and point within 50 m (or the same address1 without a point); exactly one distinct full UPRN in range.
  - Route b: the smallest OSM building outline containing the venue point (none: OS Open UPRN points within 10 m); in a layer with a measured constant offset the moved point is tried too; exactly one OS Open UPRN point in range.
  - Route c: as route a over GIAS, ODS and Active Places rows with UPRNs.
  - Confidence: high = route a, exact name, OS Open UPRN point of the candidate within 150 m of the venue (or of the venue moved by its layer offset); medium = other route a, route c, route b with the candidate within 1 m of the venue's own point in an outline of at most 25 UPRN points; low = other route b. High and medium applied; low recorded, not applied.
  - Two or more candidates: never chosen between; the venue stays unknown with the candidates listed.
- Inputs:
  - [feeds/london-datastore/cultural-infrastructure/cultural-infrastructure.geojson](feeds/london-datastore/cultural-infrastructure/cultural-infrastructure.geojson)
  - [feeds/london-datastore/catalogue.json](feeds/london-datastore/catalogue.json)
  - **lds-23697** (London Datastore 23697: Cultural Infrastructure Map 2023, OGL v3.0): https://data.london.gov.uk/download/23697/<resource id>/<file>; GET every CSV, zip and venue GeoPackage of the dataset (all revisions; CEZ files skipped)
  - **lds-2ko88** (London Datastore 2ko88: Cultural Infrastructure Map, OGL v3.0): https://data.london.gov.uk/download/2ko88/<resource id>/<file>; GET every CSV, zip and venue GeoPackage (the 2018-2020 releases)
  - **lds-2zj1y** (London Datastore 2zj1y: Cultural Infrastructure Map 2024, OGL v3.0): https://data.london.gov.uk/download/2zj1y/<resource id>/<file>; GET the 2024 LGBTQ+ venues CSV
  - **gla-cim-arcgis** (GLA ArcGIS map service behind the public Cultural Infrastructure Map app, not stated on the service; it serves the 2023 Cultural Infrastructure Map that the London Datastore publishes under OGL v3.0 (lds-23697)): https://gis.london.gov.uk/arcgis/rest/services/apps/Cultural_infrastructure_2023_for_webapp_verified/MapServer/<layer>/query; GET each of 37 layers, zone envelope in EPSG:27700, outFields=*, outSR=4326, f=geojson, pages of 1,000
  - `data/raw/registry/osopenuprn_202609_csv.zip` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/docklands/osm-clip.json.gz` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/gias.json](registry/sources/registers/gias.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/ods.json](registry/sources/registers/ods.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/active-places.json](registry/sources/registers/active-places.json)
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [feeds/london-datastore/cultural-infrastructure/cultural-infrastructure.uprn-amended.geojson](feeds/london-datastore/cultural-infrastructure/cultural-infrastructure.uprn-amended.geojson)
  - [feeds/london-datastore/cultural-infrastructure/cultural-infrastructure.uprn-amendments.json](feeds/london-datastore/cultural-infrastructure/cultural-infrastructure.uprn-amendments.json)
  - `data/raw/uprn-amend/` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: transform; after: `walk-london-datastore`
- Hand judgement: The thresholds (50 m, 150 m, 10 m, 1 m, 25 UPRN points) and the confidence levels are hand choices, recorded in the tool header constants and in skills/cwplans-london-datastore/SKILL.md "Amending rounded UPRNs"; the measured precision that supports them is in the amendments file meta.validation. The 2025 Cultural Infrastructure Map (2rj5o, no licence on the Datastore) was left out by decision.
- Faults: [F22](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F25](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 67. `check-feeds` (feeds/check-feeds.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/feeds/check-feeds.mjs [source ids] [--json] [--live]`
- Method: Re-checks every catalogued feed and prints its HTTP status and CORS answer, and where it differs from feeds.json.
- Rules:
  - An api is fetched only when the source has no sample; CORS counts when Access-Control-Allow-Origin is * or the site origin.
  - Network: 6 workers, but at most one request at a time to a host and 1 s between requests to the same host, 30 s timeout, User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1, Origin https://danbri.github.io on api and sample requests; only the first chunk of each body is read. Nothing is written.
- Inputs:
  - [feeds/feeds.json](feeds/feeds.json)
  - **feed-providers** (the publishers of the feeds and APIs listed in feeds/feeds.json and feeds/events.json, per entry in feeds.json): every url, api and sample URL in feeds/feeds.json; HTTP GET with Origin https://danbri.github.io; status, content type, CORS
- Outputs: none (prints only)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: check; after: nothing
- Faults: [F11](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 68. `check-events` (feeds/check-events.mjs)

- Command: `node cwplans/feeds/check-events.mjs [source ids] [--json] [--live] [--url URL]`
- Method: Re-checks the event and real-time feeds and prints status, CORS, item count, newest item date and the first titles.
- Rules:
  - Item counts come from generic parsers and can differ from the hand-tuned samples in events.json.
  - Network: 6 workers, but at most one request at a time to a host and 1 s between requests to the same host, 30 s timeout, User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; a second request with the Origin header tests CORS. Nothing is written.
- Inputs:
  - [feeds/events.json](feeds/events.json)
  - **feed-providers** (the publishers of the feeds and APIs listed in feeds/feeds.json and feeds/events.json, per entry in feeds.json): every event and real-time feed URL in feeds/events.json; HTTP GET, with and without the Origin header; items and newest date by small generic parsers (ICS, RSS, Atom, JSON, schema.org Event JSON-LD)
- Outputs: none (prints only)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: check; after: nothing
- Faults: [F11](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 69. `fetch-live` (tools/fetch-live.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node cwplans/tools/fetch-live.mjs [bikes lifts crowding jamcams ukpn overflows notams helicopters] [--no-fetch]`
- Method: Fetches live and fast-changing state for the Docklands zone (the model box plus the east margin of fetch-works) and writes one dated snapshot per source with a common item shape {id, kind, name, zone, time, position, values, url}: Santander Cycles docks, TfL lift outages, live station busyness, JamCam cameras, UKPN power cuts, Thames Water storm overflows, NOTAMs (cranes and temporary areas) and the UK AIP helicopter structure (H4 and restricted areas).
- Rules:
  - Zone: BOX_WGS84 of fetch-docklands.mjs (-0.095, 51.474, 0.015, 51.522) = 'model'; 0.015 to 0.085 E, 51.495 to 51.522 N = 'east'; items outside are dropped, except context items in helicopters.json (zone null).
  - Bikes: values as published (NbBikes, NbStandardBikes, NbEBikes, NbEmptyDocks, NbDocks; Installed, Locked, Temporary); unavailable_docks = NbDocks - NbBikes - NbEmptyDocks; time = the newest property 'modified'.
  - Lifts: stationUniqueId matched to StopPoint naptanId, then hubNaptanCode; kept when the station is in the zone; message kept as published.
  - Crowding: Tube and DLR (940GZZLU, 940GZZDL) then 910G station naptans in the zone, one at a time; percentageOfBaseline rounded to 0.001; dataAvailable false and 404 counted, not listed.
  - JamCams: position, view, available, imageUrl and videoUrl recorded; no image fetched or stored.
  - UKPN: kept when geopoint is in the zone; fullpostcodedata, mainmessage, plannedincidentreason and incidentcategorycustomerfriendlydescription dropped.
  - Storm overflows: Status 1 discharging (indicated, not confirmed), 0 not, -1 monitor offline; epoch milliseconds to UTC ISO.
  - NOTAMs: kept when a position stated in item E (DDMMSS(.s)N DDDMMSS(.s)E/W) lies in the zone, or item E states none and the Q-line centre does with a radius of at most 5 NM; deduplicated by series/number/year; kept: Q-code, FIR, item A, validity (PERM kept as text), lower/upper FL, Q radius, item E positions and 'n FT AGL / m FT AMSL' heights, area vertices for 'BOUNDED BY', the first bracketed place label; dropped: item E text, phone numbers, contacts.
  - Helicopters: H4 rows of EGLL AD 2.22 para 12 (the table anchored on 'Isle-of-Dogs'): reporting type (compulsory or on request), name, OS grid reference, position, description cut to 120 characters; the H4 vertical profile as printed; ENR 5.1 EGR158, EGR159, EGR160: vertices, arc centres and radii, upper limit, permitted users, SI; an area is in the zone when its vertex centroid or a vertex is.
  - Network: User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; one request at a time per host, at least 1.5 s apart; up to 5 tries with Retry-After or a doubling pause on 429, 5xx, timeouts and resets; 404 returned without retry; files overwritten on every run.
- Inputs:
  - **tfl-live-feeds** (Transport for London Unified API live feeds: BikePoint, TfL open data terms (Transport Data Service; OGL-based, attribution required); the terms page refuses scripted requests: re-check before production): https://api.tfl.gov.uk/BikePoint; every dock, one request
  - **tfl-live-feeds** (Transport for London Unified API live feeds: BikePoint, TfL open data terms (Transport Data Service; OGL-based, attribution required); the terms page refuses scripted requests: re-check before production): https://api.tfl.gov.uk/Disruptions/Lifts/v2/; every current lift outage, one request
  - **tfl-live-feeds** (Transport for London Unified API live feeds: BikePoint, TfL open data terms (Transport Data Service; OGL-based, attribution required); the terms page refuses scripted requests: re-check before production): https://api.tfl.gov.uk/StopPoint/Mode/tube,dlr,elizabeth-line,overground; station positions, naptan and hub codes; cached (reused when present)
  - **tfl-live-feeds** (Transport for London Unified API live feeds: BikePoint, TfL open data terms (Transport Data Service; OGL-based, attribution required); the terms page refuses scripted requests: re-check before production): https://api.tfl.gov.uk/crowding/{naptan}/Live; one request per Tube, DLR, Elizabeth line and Overground station naptan in the zone (65 on 2026-10-04)
  - **tfl-live-feeds** (Transport for London Unified API live feeds: BikePoint, TfL open data terms (Transport Data Service; OGL-based, attribution required); the terms page refuses scripted requests: re-check before production): https://api.tfl.gov.uk/Place/Type/JamCam; every camera, one request
  - **ukpn-live-faults** (UK Power Networks Open Data Portal, dataset ukpn-live-faults, CC BY 4.0 (dataset metadata, read 2026-10-04)): https://ukpowernetworks.opendatasoft.com/api/explore/v2.1/catalog/datasets/ukpn-live-faults/exports/json; the whole dataset
  - **thames-water-storm-overflows** (Thames Water Storm Overflow Activity, CC BY 4.0 (item licenceInfo, read 2026-10-04)): https://services2.arcgis.com/g6o32ZDQ33GpCIu3/arcgis/rest/services/Thames_Water_Storm_Overflow_Activity_(Production)_view/FeatureServer/0/query; envelope -0.095,51.474,0.085,51.522 (inSR 4326), outFields=*, returnGeometry=false
  - **nats-pib** (NATS UK AIS contingency full UK PIB, © NATS Limited; no open licence stated. Facts only (NOTAM id, Q-line code, positions, heights, validity, area vertices, short place label); no NOTAM text, contacts or phone numbers kept. Re-check before production): https://pibs.nats.co.uk/operational/pibs/PIB.xml; the full UK PIB, one request
  - **nats-uk-aip** (UK AIP, Crown copyright / NATS; no open licence stated. Facts only (names, grid references, positions, altitudes, limits); no tables, charts or text copied. Re-check against the current issue before production): https://www.aurora.nats.co.uk/htmlAIP/Publications/2024-03-21-AIRAC/html/eAIP/EG-AD-2.EGLL-en-GB.html and EG-ENR-5.1-en-GB.html; two pages; cached (reused when present)
  - `data/raw/live/ (raw responses; --no-fetch reads them)` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [feeds/live/bikes.json](feeds/live/bikes.json)
  - [feeds/live/lifts.json](feeds/live/lifts.json)
  - [feeds/live/crowding.json](feeds/live/crowding.json)
  - [feeds/live/jamcams.json](feeds/live/jamcams.json)
  - [feeds/live/ukpn.json](feeds/live/ukpn.json)
  - [feeds/live/overflows.json](feeds/live/overflows.json)
  - [feeds/live/notams.json](feeds/live/notams.json)
  - [feeds/live/helicopters.json](feeds/live/helicopters.json)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Hand judgement: Which sources are open enough to commit (TfL terms, CC BY 4.0) and which only as facts (NATS PIB, UK AIP) or not at all (CAA CAP 1455; OpenSky, adsb.fi, airplanes.live, ADS-B Exchange; adsb.lol as ODbL without the owner's agreement) is recorded in data-register.json sources, feeds/live/README.md and the skill cwplans-live-state.

#### 83. `walk-portals` (tools/walk-portals.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node cwplans/tools/walk-portals.mjs <dgu|pdg|boroughs|nomis|onsgeo|national> walk [--refresh]; node cwplans/tools/walk-portals.mjs <portal> triage; NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node cwplans/tools/walk-portals.mjs <portal> harvest [key ...]; node cwplans/tools/walk-portals.mjs dgu catalogue; node cwplans/tools/walk-portals.mjs index`
- Method: Walks the open-data portals other than the London Datastore that cover the zone, one adapter per portal (tools/portals/*.mjs): reads each catalogue whole (data.gov.uk CKAN: all 59,451 datasets; planning.data.gov.uk: all 222 datasets with a count of the entities that meet the zone; the six zone borough portals; Nomis Census 2021 bulk tables; the ONS Open Geography Portal; the national sources of the brief), triages every dataset in scope by written rules to one final state (harvested, held, listed-for-harvest, deferred, not-relevant, not-open, unavailable, sensitive, walked-elsewhere), and harvests the open, relevant datasets clipped to the 3D model box as GeoJSON (WGS84) or JSON rows with a meta member (source, API, fetch date, licence, attribution, method, counts).
- Rules:
  - Politeness: one request at a time over the run, at least 1.1 s apart, robots.txt read for every host before the first request and obeyed (longest matching rule), backoff on 429 and 5xx with Retry-After, the project User-Agent.
  - Licence class (licenceClass in walk-portals.mjs): OGL, CC BY, ODC-By, CC0/PDDL/public domain are open; NC or closed is restricted; CC BY-SA and ODbL are share-alike (never harvested; OSM is the only share-alike exception); 'no conditions apply' alone is open-unclear; no licence is none (metadata only). data.gov.uk records with no licence whose resources all sit on GOV.UK, ONS, Nomis, NHS Digital, DfT road traffic or data.police.uk count as ogl-site-terms (those sites' terms give OGL v3.0).
  - data.gov.uk scope: S1 local publishers (zone boroughs, LTGDC, Canal & River Trust, TfL, Royal Museums Greenwich, PLA, local NHS), S2 named national publishers, S3 the GLA (the London Datastore: state walked-elsewhere), S4 other publishers whose title or tags name a zone place or borough; out of scope only counted per organisation.
  - data.gov.uk states, first match: T0 by hand; T1 sensitive title; T2 held (dataset id, or a name of 15+ characters with a hyphen, in a tool, data-register.json or pipeline.json); T3 walked-elsewhere (GLA); T4 unavailable (no resources); T5 not-relevant administrative (payments, spending, staff, contracts, FOI); T6 not-open; T7 not-relevant other area (bounding box misses the zone, or the title names another region, county, city or London borough); T8 deferred documents only; T9 listed-for-harvest (zone place, zone borough, London-fine or national-fine); T10 not-relevant coarser than a borough; T11 deferred area unknown.
  - Zone place names (title and tags only, not the description): the London Datastore list plus Thames Barrier, Tideway, Lower Lea, Leamouth; not when the text has tree or crop words (Poplar is also a tree: 'Energy Crops Scheme' and short rotation coppice records matched it).
  - planning.data.gov.uk: zone = entities whose geometry intersects the box polygon (the platform's spatial query); for documents, agreements and plans the entities of the zone planning organisations (GLA 144, Greenwich 150, Lewisham 198, City 203, Newham 246, Southwark 329, Tower Hamlets 350). States P0 by hand (held from another source), P1 harvested, P2 ended, P3 not-open, P4 empty, P5 code list, P6 nothing in the zone, P7 listed-for-harvest, P8 deferred.
  - Harvest: whole geometries that meet the box are kept, rounded to 6 decimals, with in_zone, whole_in_zone, in_cw; EA flood zones are simplified (0.5 m) and cut at the box (clipped_to_zone). Fields matching e-mail, phone, contact, owner name, person, officer, agent, applicant are dropped and listed.
  - Committed outputs stay small: the data.gov.uk triage lists datasets one by one only for the states that need a decision or hold data; the bulk states are name lists keyed by state, rule and reason. National files stay raw and uncommitted; only zone extracts are committed.
  - Borough portals: City of London map service layers with the box envelope count; licence per layer from the City's data.gov.uk INSPIRE record (F42); layers about people or their sites deferred (B2); Southwark and Greenwich observatories read from the Esri UK InstantAtlas master tables and metadata service (indicators of national publishers are walked-elsewhere, B3); Lewisham licence from each JKAN source file; states B0-B8.
  - Nomis: robots.txt disallows the API; the Census 2021 bulk zips are read, the OA CSV streamed, zone OAs (zone-codes.json oa21) kept; states N1-N3.
  - ONS Open Geography: ArcGIS search catalogue; states G1-G8 by product family from the title; lookups read by code list in batches of 60.
  - National: a hand-checked source list (licence, robots, state); STATS19 and police.uk kept as aggregates only (LSOA x year x severity; snap point x category, month x category), no record-level rows; STATS19 LSOA codes matched as 2011 and 2021 (F44); DESNZ workbooks read sheet by sheet, the header row is a short cell naming an LSOA/MSOA code.
- Inputs:
  - **op-dgu-catalogue** (data.gov.uk catalogue, catalogue metadata OGL v3.0 (data.gov.uk); each dataset has its own licence, recorded per record): https://www.data.gov.uk/api/action/package_search; walk: GET ?rows=1000&start=<n>&sort=metadata_created asc (60 pages, 59,451 datasets on 2026-10-04)
  - **op-pdg-catalogue** (planning.data.gov.uk, OGL v3.0 (every dataset; attribution text per dataset)): https://www.planning.data.gov.uk/dataset.json; walk: GET dataset.json, then one entity.json count per dataset (zone polygon, intersects; or per zone planning organisation)
  - **op-nomis-bulk** (Nomis, OGL v3.0 (ONS Census 2021)): https://www.nomisweb.co.uk/sources/census_2021_bulk; walk: GET the bulk page; harvest: GET census2021-<table>.zip (the Nomis API is disallowed by robots.txt and not used)
  - **op-onsgeo-catalogue** (ONS Open Geography Portal item catalogue, OGL v3.0 (https://www.ons.gov.uk/methodology/geography/licences)): https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/search?q=orgid:ESMARspQHYMw9BZ9; walk: ArcGIS sharing search, 100 items a page (27 pages); harvest: FeatureServer query (envelope or code list)
  - **op-boroughs-catalogue** (Zone borough portals: City of London INSPIRE map service, Tower Hamlets ArcGIS Hub, Southwark and Greenwich observatories, per layer or dataset, recorded in the catalogue (City layers: their data.gov.uk INSPIRE records; Tower Hamlets hub: no licence named; observatory indicators: the Rights field; Lewisham: OGL v3.0 per dataset)): https://www.mapping.cityoflondon.gov.uk/arcgis/rest/services/INSPIRE/MapServer; walk: City of London map service layers and zone counts; Tower Hamlets ArcGIS Hub DCAT feed; Southwark and Greenwich InstantAtlas master tables and the InstantAtlas metadata service; Open Data Lewisham datasets.json and dataset files
  - **op-national-list** (National sources of the brief, checked by hand, per source, recorded in feeds/portals/national/catalogue.json): https://roadtraffic.dft.gov.uk/; harvest: DfT AADF CSVs per local authority; STATS19 collisions CSV (streamed); police.uk crimes-street API (12 months x 6 tiles); DESNZ LSOA/MSOA workbooks
  - **op-bor-city-airport-safeguarding-area** (City of London INSPIRE map service: Airport Safeguarding Area, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.mapping.cityoflondon.gov.uk/arcgis/rest/services/INSPIRE/MapServer/106/query?where=1=1&geometry=<zone box>&geometryType=esriGeometryEnvelope&inSR=4326&spatialRel=esriSpatialRelIntersects&outFields=*&outSR=4326&f=geojson&resultOffset=<n>; boroughs harvest city-airport-safeguarding-area
  - **op-bor-city-city-flood-risk-area** (City of London INSPIRE map service: City Flood Risk Area, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.mapping.cityoflondon.gov.uk/arcgis/rest/services/INSPIRE/MapServer/109/query?where=1=1&geometry=<zone box>&geometryType=esriGeometryEnvelope&inSR=4326&spatialRel=esriSpatialRelIntersects&outFields=*&outSR=4326&f=geojson&resultOffset=<n>; boroughs harvest city-city-flood-risk-area
  - **op-bor-city-coal-tax-posts** (City of London INSPIRE map service: Coal Tax Posts, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.mapping.cityoflondon.gov.uk/arcgis/rest/services/INSPIRE/MapServer/3/query?where=1=1&geometry=<zone box>&geometryType=esriGeometryEnvelope&inSR=4326&spatialRel=esriSpatialRelIntersects&outFields=*&outSR=4326&f=geojson&resultOffset=<n>; boroughs harvest city-coal-tax-posts
  - **op-bor-city-crossrail-safeguarding-area** (City of London INSPIRE map service: Crossrail Safeguarding Area, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.mapping.cityoflondon.gov.uk/arcgis/rest/services/INSPIRE/MapServer/110/query?where=1=1&geometry=<zone box>&geometryType=esriGeometryEnvelope&inSR=4326&spatialRel=esriSpatialRelIntersects&outFields=*&outSR=4326&f=geojson&resultOffset=<n>; boroughs harvest city-crossrail-safeguarding-area
  - **op-bor-city-cultural-spaces** (City of London INSPIRE map service: Cultural Spaces, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.mapping.cityoflondon.gov.uk/arcgis/rest/services/INSPIRE/MapServer/175/query?where=1=1&geometry=<zone box>&geometryType=esriGeometryEnvelope&inSR=4326&spatialRel=esriSpatialRelIntersects&outFields=*&outSR=4326&f=json (Esri rings converted)&resultOffset=<n>; boroughs harvest city-cultural-spaces
  - **op-bor-city-cycleways** (City of London INSPIRE map service: Cycleways, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.mapping.cityoflondon.gov.uk/arcgis/rest/services/INSPIRE/MapServer/160/query?where=1=1&geometry=<zone box>&geometryType=esriGeometryEnvelope&inSR=4326&spatialRel=esriSpatialRelIntersects&outFields=*&outSR=4326&f=geojson&resultOffset=<n>; boroughs harvest city-cycleways
  - **op-bor-city-grit-bins** (City of London INSPIRE map service: Grit Bins, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.mapping.cityoflondon.gov.uk/arcgis/rest/services/INSPIRE/MapServer/48/query?where=1=1&geometry=<zone box>&geometryType=esriGeometryEnvelope&inSR=4326&spatialRel=esriSpatialRelIntersects&outFields=*&outSR=4326&f=geojson&resultOffset=<n>; boroughs harvest city-grit-bins
  - **op-bor-city-highway-authority-subway** (City of London INSPIRE map service: Highway Authority Subway, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.mapping.cityoflondon.gov.uk/arcgis/rest/services/INSPIRE/MapServer/111/query?where=1=1&geometry=<zone box>&geometryType=esriGeometryEnvelope&inSR=4326&spatialRel=esriSpatialRelIntersects&outFields=*&outSR=4326&f=geojson&resultOffset=<n>; boroughs harvest city-highway-authority-subway
  - **op-bor-city-historic-land-use** (City of London INSPIRE map service: Historic Land Use, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.mapping.cityoflondon.gov.uk/arcgis/rest/services/INSPIRE/MapServer/113/query?where=1=1&geometry=<zone box>&geometryType=esriGeometryEnvelope&inSR=4326&spatialRel=esriSpatialRelIntersects&outFields=*&outSR=4326&f=geojson&resultOffset=<n>; boroughs harvest city-historic-land-use
  - **op-bor-city-lvmf-protected-vistas** (City of London INSPIRE map service: LVMF Protected Vistas, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.mapping.cityoflondon.gov.uk/arcgis/rest/services/INSPIRE/MapServer/116/query?where=1=1&geometry=<zone box>&geometryType=esriGeometryEnvelope&inSR=4326&spatialRel=esriSpatialRelIntersects&outFields=*&outSR=4326&f=geojson&resultOffset=<n>; boroughs harvest city-lvmf-protected-vistas

  - `data/raw/portals/` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Hand judgement: The scope lists, the zone place exceptions, the JUDGED tables (held from another source, coarser than the zone) and the HARVEST tables were set by hand from the first triage; the reasons are in the tables and in feeds/portals/README.md. Licences that read 'no restrictions' without naming a licence (the Tower Hamlets ArcGIS Hub) are not treated as open.
- Faults: [F42](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F43](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F44](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F45](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 84. `find-kml` (tools/find-kml.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 KML_DOM_DIR=<dir with @xmldom/xmldom and linkedom> node cwplans/tools/find-kml.mjs <copy [id ...]|probe|all>`
- Method: Answers the owner's question 'Can you find any kml resources for the area?' (2026-10-05). A hand list of candidates, found by searching the project's own catalogues (London Datastore resources with format kml/kmz, the raw data.gov.uk walk: 4,499 of 59,451 datasets have a KML resource) and the open-data hubs of TfL, Walk Wheel Cycle Trust and Canal & River Trust, is fetched as the 3D page would fetch it (Origin header of the Pages site) and read with the page's own reader docklands/kml.js (a DOMParser from @xmldom/xmldom, description HTML through linkedom). For each: HTTP status, https, the CORS header, the counts of placemarks in the zone and in the model box, unsupported elements. Open layers that cannot be opened by URL (no KML, or a whole-London file) are written as zone-clipped KML with the page's writer into cwplans/feeds/kml/, with the source, licence and attribution in the Document description. Result: feeds/kml/catalogue.json and README.md.
- Rules:
  - Politeness: one request at a time, at least 1.1 s apart, robots.txt read per host with walk-portals.mjs allowed() and obeyed, the project User-Agent.
  - Licence class as walk-portals.mjs licenceClass; only ogl, cc-by, odc-by and public-domain layers are copied or offered as examples; share-alike (except OSM content inside an open layer, marked odbl and osm derived), restricted and no-licence resources are listed with the reason.
  - cors yes = HTTP 200 over https and Access-Control-Allow-Origin * or https://danbri.github.io; a ?kml= link is made only for a file the reader parsed with at least one placemark in the zone.
  - Zone = the model box plus the Royal Docks and the Thames Barrier, WGS84 -0.095, 51.47, 0.08, 51.53; copies keep features that meet the box (polygons whole, lines cut at the box edge with one vertex beyond), the names and a few fields.
  - The copies are pushed to danbri/londat before the probe, which then reads them from raw.githubusercontent.com; the headless page test of every ?kml= link is stored in the catalogue as page_test.
- Inputs:
  - **kml-catalogue** (KML and KMZ candidates for the zone, metadata only (titles, URLs, licence words, HTTP status, CORS header, feature counts); each resource keeps its own licence, recorded per entry)
  - **kml-tfl-gis-hub** (Transport for London GIS Open Data Hub feature layers, Open Government Licence v3.0 (the licence of each ArcGIS item))
  - **kml-sustrans-ncn** (National Cycle Network, Open Government Licence v3.0 (item licence); contains OS data (Crown copyright 2018) and OpenStreetMap contributions (ODbL))
  - **kml-crt-locks** (Canal & River Trust locks, Open Government Licence (item licence))
  - **lds-2jx1l** (London Datastore: Create your own mapping templates, Open Government Licence v2)
  - [feeds/portals/dgu/national-trails-england3/national-trails-england3.geojson](feeds/portals/dgu/national-trails-england3/national-trails-england3.geojson)
  - [feeds/portals/pdg/listed-building/listed-building.geojson](feeds/portals/pdg/listed-building/listed-building.geojson)
  - [feeds/portals/pdg/world-heritage-site/world-heritage-site.geojson](feeds/portals/pdg/world-heritage-site/world-heritage-site.geojson)
  - [feeds/portals/pdg/world-heritage-site-buffer-zone/world-heritage-site-buffer-zone.geojson](feeds/portals/pdg/world-heritage-site-buffer-zone/world-heritage-site-buffer-zone.geojson)
  - [feeds/portals/pdg/heritage-at-risk/heritage-at-risk.geojson](feeds/portals/pdg/heritage-at-risk/heritage-at-risk.geojson)
  - [feeds/portals/pdg/park-and-garden/park-and-garden.geojson](feeds/portals/pdg/park-and-garden/park-and-garden.geojson)
  - [feeds/london-datastore/conservation-areas/conservation-areas.geojson](feeds/london-datastore/conservation-areas/conservation-areas.geojson)
  - [docklands/kml.js](docklands/kml.js)
- Outputs:
  - [feeds/kml/catalogue.json](feeds/kml/catalogue.json)
  - [feeds/kml/tfl-cycle-routes.kml](feeds/kml/tfl-cycle-routes.kml)
  - [feeds/kml/tfl-river-piers.kml](feeds/kml/tfl-river-piers.kml)
  - [feeds/kml/tfl-river-services.kml](feeds/kml/tfl-river-services.kml)
  - [feeds/kml/sustrans-ncn.kml](feeds/kml/sustrans-ncn.kml)
  - [feeds/kml/crt-locks.kml](feeds/kml/crt-locks.kml)
  - [feeds/kml/ne-thames-path.kml](feeds/kml/ne-thames-path.kml)
  - [feeds/kml/he-listed-buildings.kml](feeds/kml/he-listed-buildings.kml)
  - [feeds/kml/he-world-heritage.kml](feeds/kml/he-world-heritage.kml)
  - [feeds/kml/he-heritage-at-risk.kml](feeds/kml/he-heritage-at-risk.kml)
