## Libraries and tests

- [tools/ea-tide-archive.mjs](tools/ea-tide-archive.mjs): EA tide archive: the three tide gauges round the 3D model (0007 Tower Pier, 0003 Charlton, 0001 Silvertown), the cached, retried, streamed fetch of one EA daily archive file (readings-YYYY-MM-DD.csv, all stations, about 60 MB, OGL v3.0) filtered to their 15-minute tidal level measures, and the reader of the cached lines (data/raw/river/tide/). Used by fit-tide-harmonics and build-tide-history.
- [tools/lidar-roofs.py](tools/lidar-roofs.py): Roof fits (numpy, tifffile): the EA LiDAR DSM and DTM tile zips as one mosaic; per low model building the 1 m cells inside its outline (0.75 m inset) minus the model ground, and flat, gable (ridge along the long or the short side, ridge offset searched) and hipped fits in the outline's minimum-area rectangle, with their errors. Used by build-roofs.
- [tools/facade-tile.py](tools/facade-tile.py): Facade tiles (Pillow): a 256 px cut of a rectified patch (a mirrored half for a symmetric face), a vector pattern drawn in metres to PNG and SVG, and the composed 8 x 4 atlas. Used by contrib-photos (cut-facade-tiles) and compose-facade-atlas.
- [tools/kgx-ops.mjs](tools/kgx-ops.mjs): The kgx dataflow runtime (skill cwplans-dataflow): Flow (file inputs named by SHA-256, graph versions named by RDFC-1.0 hash, memoised operation runs, log/activities.jsonl and log/versions.jsonl; every IRI an ID from kgx-ids.mjs), OPS.partition (partition-by-subject-key), OPS.mapLegacyIds and mapLegacyVersion (map-legacy-ids), hasLegacyNames (a version with old names or old vocabulary terms), mapQuad, quadsOf (Factoidal parse a chunk of lines at a time), zoneKey and partitionLines. Used by build-kgx and the tools that write graph versions.
- [tools/kgx-ids.mjs](tools/kgx-ids.mjs): The knowledge-graph IDs (skill cwplans-kgx): ID_BASE https://kgx.foaf.tv/id/, an() (lowercase alphanumeric form), the code table per kind, kid(kind, ...parts) (mint an ID from the parts of the old name), mapLegacy(iri) (a name of the old namespaces to its ID, any other IRI unchanged, an old name with no code throws), the registry that fails when two names give one ID, cwgPath, osmKeyOf, the vocabularies VOCAB (cwk: https://kgx.foaf.tv/vocab#), REGISTER_VOCAB (cwp: https://kgx.foaf.tv/pipeline#), IDIOMS_VOCAB (https://kgx.foaf.tv/idioms#) and VOCAB_MOVES (an old vocabulary term to the new namespace, same local name; since 2026-10-07); --test runs the self-test. Used by kgx-ops, build-kgx, check-data-register, key-model-buildings, contrib-photos, compose-facade-atlas and probe-imagery-coverage.
- [tools/londat.mjs](tools/londat.mjs): Paths of the project in this repository (since 2026-10-07 one checkout holds everything): LONDAT_DIR (env; default the checkout that holds the tool), LONDAT_CW (cwplans/), cwPath(rel) (the file on disk), and HOSTED_DIRS and isHostedPath(rel) (the folders that were in danbri/londat while the rest was in danbri/glitchcan-minigam, 2026-10-05 to 2026-10-07; kept for the tools that name them). Used by walk-london-datastore, lds-harvest-auto, join-lds, amend-uprns, build-construction-index, walk-portals and the tools that write kgx/, data/images/ and third_party/.
- [tools/lib.mjs](tools/lib.mjs): HTTP get with the project User-Agent; QLever (paced, one call at a time, retried on 429 and 5xx) and query.wikidata.org SPARQL helpers; EA LiDAR WCS URLs; WGS84 to BNG through the OSTN15 grid (reads data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif); LiDAR tile mosaics; polygon helpers (area, point in polygon, box clip, cells, earcut mesh, ring joining, Douglas-Peucker, delta encoding); applyControls (published levels from data/sourced-levels.json as tunnel controls).
- [tools/registry-lib.mjs](tools/registry-lib.mjs): Paths for registry/sources and data/raw/registry; the Canary Wharf and Docklands boxes; cwPostcodes() (reads postcodes/postcodes.json; tiers cw-core, cw-ward, cw-box-other-ward, and terminated-cw unless told not to); postcode normaliser; CSV line splitter; sleep; inBox.
- [tools/osm-values.mjs](tools/osm-values.mjs): Parsers for OSM tag values: osmList (';' lists), unitLabel (adds 'Unit' to a bare designator), osmLevels (lists, comma lists, ranges, fractions) and osmLevelsUnread (tokens it cannot read).
- [registry/sources/brands/tools/lib.mjs](registry/sources/brands/tools/lib.mjs): Paths for the brands source (OUT, CW, RAW, NSI_DIR), the Canary Wharf box, One Canada Square, User-Agent, a require for the NSI packages installed in data/raw/registry/nsi, readJSON, sleep, inBox.
- [registry/sources/brands/tools/nsi-matcher.mjs](registry/sources/brands/tools/nsi-matcher.mjs): At import, builds the NSI Matcher over the brands/* tree with its location index (reads data/raw/registry/nsi/.../dist/json/nsi.json and featureCollection.json) and loads registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json; exports matcher, brands, byId, byWd, KVS, hits(), nameVariants(), matchAny().
- [registry/sources/brands/tools/cwg-fields.mjs](registry/sources/brands/tools/cwg-fields.mjs): cwgFields (mall, level, postcode, district, street line and flags from CWG address lines), cwgLevelNum, nameKeys (name keys for joins: no accents or punctuation, "&" as "and", branch suffixes that name a place removed), CWG_PLACES, CWG_MALLS.
- [tools/structured-dom.mjs](tools/structured-dom.mjs): extractStructuredData(): self-contained in-page extractor for Playwright page.evaluate: JSON-LD script texts as found, HTML microdata as JSON (WHATWG algorithm, itemref followed), RDFa 1.1 (Core + HTML+RDFa rules) as N-Triples with the document base.
- [tools/jsonld-clean.mjs](tools/jsonld-clean.mjs): cleanJsonLd(raw): repairs one real-world JSON-LD block and counts each repair by class (HTML comment and CDATA wrappers, JS comments, trailing and missing commas, raw control characters, invalid escapes, concatenated values, HTML entities, remote schema.org contexts inlined, other remote contexts dropped, @graph unwrapped, http://schema.org/ normalised).
- [tools/native/ecw2ppm.c](tools/native/ecw2ppm.c): ECW decoder for build-aerial.py: decodes an ECW file to a PPM at every Nth pixel and prints its georeferencing as JSON. Built by hand against the ECW 3.3 SDK (curation skill, Imagery); the binary tools/native/ecw2ppm is gitignored.
- [tools/lds-probe.mjs](tools/lds-probe.mjs): Area detection inside London Datastore files for walk-london-datastore.mjs probe: the size caps, readers (CSV and text samples, sheets through SheetJS, GeoPackage envelopes, zipped shapefile envelopes and dbf), the signals (coordinates, UPRNs, postcodes, OA/LSOA/MSOA/ward/borough codes, borough and zone place names) and the area classes D1 to D8. No side effects; the caller passes the data.
- [tools/portals/dgu.mjs](tools/portals/dgu.mjs): walk-portals.mjs adapter for data.gov.uk: CKAN walk to a compact raw catalogue, scope S1-S4, triage rules T0-T11, the compact committed catalogue.
- [tools/portals/pdg.mjs](tools/portals/pdg.mjs): walk-portals.mjs adapter for planning.data.gov.uk: dataset list, zone counts by spatial query or by zone planning organisation, triage P0-P8, harvest (GeoJSON by spatial query; tables by organisation; simplify and clip for flood zones).
- [tools/portals/nomis.mjs](tools/portals/nomis.mjs): walk-portals.mjs adapter for the Nomis Census 2021 bulk page and zips (the API is disallowed by robots.txt): catalogue of tables, triage N1-N3, harvest of zone output-area rows streamed from the zips.
- [tools/portals/onsgeo.mjs](tools/portals/onsgeo.mjs): walk-portals.mjs adapter for the ONS Open Geography Portal: item catalogue by ArcGIS search, triage G1-G8 by product family, harvest of zone boundaries, centroids and the OA lookup by ArcGIS query.
- [tools/portals/boroughs.mjs](tools/portals/boroughs.mjs): walk-portals.mjs adapter for the zone borough portals: City of London map service (licence per layer from its data.gov.uk records), Tower Hamlets ArcGIS Hub, Southwark and Greenwich InstantAtlas indicators, Open Data Lewisham; triage B0-B8; harvest of City layers and Lewisham EV charge points.
- [tools/portals/national.mjs](tools/portals/national.mjs): walk-portals.mjs adapter for the national sources of the brief: the hand-checked list (licence, robots, state) and harvests of DfT AADF, STATS19 aggregates, police.uk aggregates and DESNZ energy by LSOA/MSOA.
- [tools/portals/index.mjs](tools/portals/index.mjs): walk-portals.mjs index: feeds/portals/index.json, counts per portal and harvested file sizes.
- [tools/test/building-models.test.mjs](tools/test/building-models.test.mjs) (test): Tests (node --test) of docklands/data/building-models.json against area.js: the model fingerprint, indices, triangle counts, vertices inside the outline box and above the ground, heights near the model roof, and the .glb hash.
- [tools/test/roofs.test.mjs](tools/test/roofs.test.mjs) (test): Tests (node --test) of docklands/data/roofs.json against area.js (fingerprint, low buildings only, eave below ridge, ridge centre near the outline) and of the page's roof builder in docklands/roofs-layer.js (every vertex finite, inside the outline box, between the ground and the ridge; roof vertices not below the eave).
- [tools/test/materials.test.mjs](tools/test/materials.test.mjs) (test): Tests (node --test) of docklands/data/materials.json against area.js (fingerprint, one fixed-width cell a building, codes 0 to 63, palettes and uses in range, style counts) and of the page painter in docklands/look-layer.js (wall and roof colours times the shading, alpha 132 + code, the Night use added only where none).
- [tools/test/osm-values.test.mjs](tools/test/osm-values.test.mjs) (test): Fixture tests (node --test) for osmList, unitLabel, osmLevels and osmLevelsUnread in tools/osm-values.mjs.
- [tools/splat-pose-test.py](tools/splat-pose-test.py) (test): Pose-convention test for the 3DGS trainer: 'gen OUT' writes a synthetic nerfstudio dataset of coloured boxes on a chequered ground with known OpenGL camera-to-world matrices (images, transforms.json, sparse_pc.ply; --init grey, --flip opencv as a negative control); 'points OUT N' rewrites sparse_pc.ply with about N points; 'check PLY' reports per box how many opaque gaussians of a trained PLY lie inside it and their mean colour. Needs numpy and Pillow.
- [tools/test/cwg-fields.test.mjs](tools/test/cwg-fields.test.mjs) (test): Fixture tests (node --test) for cwgFields, cwgLevelNum and nameKeys in registry/sources/brands/tools/cwg-fields.mjs, including the parse faults of the first fetch-cwg ("United Kingdom" and "55 Upper Bank Street" as levels).
- [tools/test/jsonld-clean.test.mjs](tools/test/jsonld-clean.test.mjs) (test): Fixture tests (node --test) for cleanJsonLd in tools/jsonld-clean.mjs, including the bigeasy.co.uk missing-comma block of 2026-10-03.
- [tools/test/kgx-ids.test.mjs](tools/test/kgx-ids.test.mjs) (test): The self-test of tools/kgx-ids.mjs under node --test: the code-table examples (old name -> ID), idempotence of mapLegacy, old vocabulary terms to https://kgx.foaf.tv/ (vocab#, pipeline#, idioms#), unchanged new terms and external IRIs, the error cases (an old kind with no code, an empty rest, two names for one ID), osmKeyOf and cwgPath.
- [tools/test/tide-harmonics.test.mjs](tools/test/tide-harmonics.test.mjs) (test): Tests (node --test) of docklands/data/sky/tide-harmonics.json and the prediction in docklands/tide.js (the Three.js port's tide): the constituents, the held-out error against the EA readings of 3-4 Oct 2026, the page code agreeing with the tool's check values.
